1,011 entries, cluster-ops to code-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cluster-ops**: Kubernetes cluster state inspection, debugging, and service connectivity testing agent. Use for pod failures, Helm install errors, DNS/network issues, OIDC auth errors, and certificate problems. ([dasomel/narwhal](https://github.com/dasomel/narwhal/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cluster-ops.md) · ★3)
- **cluster-ops**: Pi K3s cluster MUTATION executor. Use to make infrastructure changes — manifest edits, GitOps/Flux deploys, git+PR, and the bounded MCP mutations (reconcile/restart/refresh/backup). Runs in the container, so it has no kubectl/op/ssh: it escalates those rather than running them. For diagnosis ("X is broken"), use cluster-diagnostics instead; this agent executes the fix. ([mtgibbs/pi-cluster](https://github.com/mtgibbs/pi-cluster/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cluster-ops.md) · ★3)
- **cluster-ops**: Load and follow the instructions in `.ai/skills/cluster-ops/SKILL.md`. ([gahan9/project_template](https://github.com/gahan9/project_template/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/cluster-ops.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **cluster-ops**: Operate the BGU SLURM cluster for the PDDL copilot sweep — check job queue + progress, sync results, run preflight, post-mortem completed jobs. Delegate here when the user asks about cluster state, job status or results sync, especially when the SSH/script output would otherwise bloat the main conversation. Aggregation, plots and tables belong to the `analyzer` skill, not this agent. Every action here uses SSH, so the parent must already have Omer's explicit go-ahead. Returns a concise summary, not raw stdout dumps. ([SPL-BGU/pddl-copilot-experiments](https://github.com/SPL-BGU/pddl-copilot-experiments/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cluster-ops.md) · ★1)
- **cluster-ops**: Owns the launch half of PREFLIGHT.md, RUN.md and cluster incidents on NRP Nautilus (namespace cms-ml). Use for lint, smoke tests, launches and incident write-ups. ([kai124138/bnjettag-oct9](https://github.com/kai124138/bnjettag-oct9/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cluster-ops.md))
- **cluster-ops**: Handbook 의 배포·Istio·Kargo·ArgoCD·관측 전문가. 런타임 클러스터 진단 및 차트 설정. oc 실행 가능. ([sayaya1090/handbook](https://github.com/sayaya1090/handbook/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/cluster-ops.md))
- **cluster-planner**: A `model: sonnet` subagent that runs a stripped-down `/z-plan`-equivalent for ONE narrow scope (one cluster) within a `/z-plan-split` run. Produces INTENT.md + MANIFEST.json for the cluster, resolves small decisions unilaterally, and escalates risky decisions back to the main thread via a structured `decision_needed` payload. Used only by `/z-plan-split`; never invoked directly by the user. ([zeke13dev/z-harness](https://github.com/zeke13dev/z-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/cluster-planner.md))
- **cluster-reader**: Judges land-cover cluster cells from satellite exemplar crops. Use when a labeling round needs per-exemplar verdicts against the land-cover hierarchy — initial judging, re-judging after a definition change, or auditing an existing pass. Reads the AOI pack's class definitions itself; give it cluster ids and a run dir. ([cyberchitta/geo-darshan](https://github.com/cyberchitta/geo-darshan/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/cluster-reader.md))
- **cluster-runner**: Cheap executor for BULK mechanical cluster work over ssh - triaging many job logs, multi-run merges/pulls, large rsync transfers, long greps over remote output. Give it exact commands or a tight brief; it reports a compact summary, it does not decide strategy. Not worth it for one or two quick commands (run those inline). ([ben-carew/Coral-Showcase](https://github.com/ben-carew/Coral-Showcase/blob/HEAD/agentic-research/agents/cluster-runner.md))
- **cluster-scan**: Free-form security analysis of a code cluster. Spawned during SCAN phase for broad cluster exploration. Receives full source, structural context, and trust assumptions. Returns findings, refutations, backlog items, and storage handoff points. ([Artifex1/auditor-addon](https://github.com/Artifex1/auditor-addon/blob/HEAD/agents/cluster-scan.md) · ★4)
